Invoke the helper through authorized execution with exactly `<logfile> <phase> <decision> <why> <evidence> <result>`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  The nonempty logfile is an explicit owned local path.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Tabs, newlines and carriage returns are rejected before writes, and formula-leading cells are quoted.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Read back the trail and retain the skill'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s scoped transcript audit, cross-model review and Attention requirements.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Append-only logging proves neither unattended execution nor recovery.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Multi-phase plans use [check-plan.mjs](scripts/check-plan.mjs) as `bun --no-install <package-root>/skills/poteto-mode/scripts/check-plan.mjs <plan.md>`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  It takes one positional path and reads without edits.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Exit 2 means usage or unreadable input, 1 means format problems, and 0 means structural success.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Keep capability declarations, live and perf evidence, independent review and exact-head receipts.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  It checks pstack plans only.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  It never validates another controller'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s plan, implementation or host behavior.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Orchestrate uses its [complete helper contract](playbooks/orchestrate.md) and the real [CLI](scripts/orch/orch.ts) and [store](scripts/orch/store.ts).\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Bind `bun --no-install <package-root>/skills/poteto-mode/scripts/orch/orch.ts --store <owned-store>` or explicit `ORCH_STORE` outside the package tree.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Use full JSON for standing orders and rows.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Preserve `inbox drain --peek --json` and actual drain receipts with durable readback before classification. `ledger check <pr> <full-head-sha>` returns NOT-VERIFIED and exit 2 for a missing key.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Usage or runtime errors exit 1.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Ledger rows replace the same key without enforcing verifier precedence.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  The caller owns precedence, append-only attempt history, exact SHA syntax, artifact inspection, terminal states, permissions and current-head filtering.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  The helper never spawns, waits or wakes workers.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Stale locks require ownership review, and `--force` requires explicit takeover permission even for a dead holder.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Frontier discovery uses local git and gt in the stack owner'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s clone and fails on unavailable metadata or pin mismatch.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Do not substitute this binding for the gt-free Autopilot stack workflow.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Atomic rename is not a crash-durability or adversarial-concurrency guarantee.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Babysit and Shipping use the GitHub-specific read-only [watcher](scripts/watch-pr/watch-pr) through their complete playbooks.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  From the package root bind `bun --no-install skills/poteto-mode/scripts/watch-pr/watch-pr` with explicit `--owner`, `--repo`, PR selection, `--head <number=full-40-character-head>` for every watched member, `--max-polls <remaining-count>` and `--timeout <remaining-seconds>`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Freeze queued order with `--queued-stack --stack-prs <bottom-to-top-list>`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Connected mode uses `--stack`, mutually exclusive with queued mode. `check` adds `--status-only`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Inspect NDJSON schemaVersion 1 kind, terminal and reason.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Exit 0 includes status-only and proves no readiness by itself.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Codes 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7 and 64 identify conflicts, threads, checks, timeout, merge gates, query failures and usage.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  A changed head, missing authentication or transport, or exhausted poll-budget blocks the result.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Queued WAITING with reason kind merge-queue is non-terminal.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Babysit reports the frontier merge-ready and stops that watcher.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Shipping waits for observed merge under its distinct rule.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Carry budgets across re-arms and retain an observed stop or deadline binding for a hard wall-clock limit.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  The helper deadline is checked between polling work.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Do not add a second sleep loop or treat GitHub output as Origin evidence.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Cleanup uses the [audit wrapper](scripts/worktree-audit.sh) and [implementation](scripts/worktree-audit.py) through its playbook with an explicit local `--repo` and verified `--base`, plus authorized `--history-map` and `--pr-state` exports when available. The helper returns logical bytes and evidence buckets, performs no fetch or deletion, and leaves unknown facts as holds. A bucket never grants deletion permission. Recheck actual usage and ignored files before separately authorized removal.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nThe [bootstrap](scripts/bootstrap.ts), [manifest](scripts/package.json) and [lock](scripts/bun.lock) require exact commander 14.0.0, bun-types 1.3.14 and typescript 7.0.2 metadata before dependency-backed CLI execution. Bootstrap fails setup-required and never installs. Run helpers with Bun runtime auto-install disabled. Missing dependencies remain blocked. The isolated tested snapshot only supports that snapshot, not this caller, the current checkout or either host. [Helper setup](../../docs/testing/helper-setup.md) states the exact-copy validation boundary and retains historical setup evidence. It does not authorize installation or transfer a historical pass to this caller. Any future setup needs fresh authorization for an exact writable copy, registry, disabled lifecycle scripts and task-owned cache. Never install in an operative invocation or bypass bootstrap.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nDelivery reads [Opening a PR](playbooks/opening-a-pr.md) in full and retains its review, companion, commit, push, draft or ready authorization and actual-result gates. A local draft is not a created PR. An exposed built-in PR tool owns operations it covers, and a failed tool does not authorize a CLI bypass. Opening alone never starts Babysit. A read-only question stays in Investigation, with no PR; its specific exception takes precedence over the generic delivery route. Prototype and planning handoffs grant no implementation or external permission. Missing create-skill, deslop, matching controls or independent review remains an explicit blocked gate even when a reduced local artifact is useful.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Writing the reply\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nWrite the reply clean as you draft it. A cleanup pass after drafting does not remove these patterns.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Short declarative sentences.** One thought per sentence, ended with a period.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **No long-dash character anywhere.** Write a file-list bullet as a sentence (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"`main.js` owns persistence and the IPC handlers\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\") and a bold section header as its own sentence (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"**Verification.** End to end via CDP\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\").\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **A colon as a mid-sentence connector is also out** (unslop rule 14). A colon before a list is fine.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Terse is not an excuse to drop content.** Short sentences, but every section the playbook'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s reply names stays: details, tradeoffs, choices, open decisions.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Frame impact for the consumer and the maintainer.** Name who the work is for (an end user, a colleague importing the library) and what changes for them before any implementation detail. Then what the next engineer who owns this code inherits. If you can'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''t say what either would notice, the work or the explanation is off.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Never fabricate a link, citation, or transcript reference.** Link only artifacts you produced or read this session.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Every claim carries its evidence or its label in the same sentence.** Measured, inferred, or guess. A prediction or an unseen cause is a guess. Never hand the human a check you could run.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nEvery playbook ends with a reply written this way. Link to the actual PR you read or created when authorized. Never claim a draft is a created PR. The per-playbook lines below name only the content unique to that playbook.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Comments\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nComments follow the same rule as the reply. Write them clean as you go. Keep a comment only for a non-obvious *why* the code can'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''t show. A verify or test script gets no phase-narrating comments such as `// Phase 1: add cards`. The assertion or log string documents the step, as in `assert(ok, '\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''persisted across restart'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\'')`. This applies to every file you produce, including the delegate'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s diff.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Playbooks\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nOpen an available todo list, or an authorized local Markdown checklist if no todo operation exists. Record that reduced tool binding. Its first items are the matched playbook'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s steps, copied in verbatim, before any task-specific todos. A step you choose not to do stays in the list with a one-line `skip: <reason>`. Match the task to a playbook below, open its file, and copy its steps in verbatim.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nA large or cross-cutting effort (a migration across many call sites, an ambitious multi-part change), or work the user steps away from to trust later, routes to the **figure-it-out** skill even when a narrower playbook like Feature fits. Use **figure-it-out** whenever no bundled playbook fits. It designs a bespoke, rigorous playbook for the task. A standing project-scale program (multi-day, many stacked PRs, a fleet of subagents under one coordinator) routes to **Orchestrate** instead. figure-it-out designs one bespoke run, orchestrate runs the program.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Investigation.** Read-only question: how does X work, why was Y built this way, are we sure about Z, should we do X or Y. `playbooks/investigation.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Bug fix.** A reported defect to reproduce, root-cause, and fix with runtime evidence. `playbooks/bug-fix.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Perf issue.** A measured slowness to trace and improve against a baseline. `playbooks/perf-issue.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Hillclimb.** Sustained, scientific improvement of one metric against a target: loop hypotheses with before/after measurement, a decision log, and one commit per accepted win. Distinct from Perf issue, which is a one-off fix. `playbooks/hillclimb.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Runtime forensics.** Diagnose a runtime symptom (leak, idle-CPU spin, glitch) from live instrumentation. The deliverable is a diagnosis, not a fix. `playbooks/runtime-forensics.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Trace forensics.** Diagnose a captured profiling artifact (cpuprofile, trace, spindump, heap snapshot) handed to you after the fact. The deliverable is a diagnosis, not a fix. `playbooks/trace-forensics.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Feature.** New or changed behavior, built from a named data shape. `playbooks/feature.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Refactoring.** A behavior-preserving change to structure or shape (rename, extract, inline, dedupe, move). `playbooks/refactoring.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Prototype.** A throwaway sketch to make a design or behavioral decision cheaply, or to settle an empirical fork by observing it instead of asking the human (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"prototype\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"mock it up\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"try this layout\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"sketch it to decide\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"). `playbooks/prototype.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Visual parity.** Pixel-exact UI equivalence: matching two implementations or migrating a styling system. `playbooks/visual-parity.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Authoring or modifying a skill.** Writing or editing a SKILL.md. `playbooks/authoring-a-skill.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Eval.** Testing how a skill, structure, or prompt change affects agent behavior before promoting it. `playbooks/eval.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Babysit.** Driving a PR or a stack to merge-ready: conflicts, review threads, CI. `playbooks/babysit.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Shipping.** The half after Babysit. Independently verifying a green stack, then landing the contiguous verified run bottom-up through `gh` by default or Origin when its CLI is available. `playbooks/shipping.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Autonomous run.** A long task to drive to completion without stopping (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"run until done\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"/loop until X\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"). `playbooks/autonomous-run.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Orchestrate.** A standing project handed to one coordinator chat: multi-day, many stacked PRs, dozens to hundreds of subagents, minimal human turns (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"run this whole project\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"own this migration until it lands\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"). Distinct from Autonomous run, which drives one task to a predicate. Work one agent could finish inside the session'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s budget routes there, not here, however program-shaped the phrasing sounds. `playbooks/orchestrate.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Autopilot-full.** A queue of independent PRs run to merged with full autonomy. One owner per PR carries build through merge, and the root swarm-verifies each PR before its owner merges (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"autopilot this queue\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"full autopilot\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", one-owner-per-PR programs). `playbooks/autopilot-full.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Autopilot-stack.** A queue of changes built and verified with full autonomy, delivered as one linear reviewed base-branch stack the operator lands (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"autopilot-stack\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"stack them, don'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''t ship\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"build the stack, I'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''ll land it\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"). `playbooks/autopilot-stack.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Session pickup.** Resuming or taking over a prior agent'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s in-flight work from a transcript, cloud-agent URL, or pushed branch. `playbooks/session-pickup.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Pause safely.** Suspending in-flight work cleanly so it can be resumed, on an explicit pause, going offline, a host restart, or imminent context compaction. The complement to Session pickup. Full steps: `playbooks/pause-safely.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Multi-phase or multi-PR plan.** Work that spans phases or stacked PRs. `playbooks/multi-phase-plan.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Worktree and simulator cleanup.** Reclaiming local disk by pruning merged or abandoned git worktrees and stale iOS simulators (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"what'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s using my disk\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"clean up worktrees\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"prune safe-to-prune worktrees\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"free up space\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"delete old simulators\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"). `playbooks/worktree-cleanup.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Opening a PR.** Invoked at the end of every other playbook. `playbooks/opening-a-pr.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\ncat: playbooks/investigation.md: No such file or directory\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n# Model selection for portable roles\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nUse [session capabilities](host-capabilities.md) before assigning a role. These [poteto](agent-prompts/poteto-agent.md) and [comment reviewer](agent-prompts/comment-sicko.md) prompts are instructions supplied to an exposed worker operation. Reading them does not register a custom agent or authorize delegation.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| Role | Default requested model | Observed model | Supported effort | Fallback disclosure |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| poteto-agent | inherit-parent | unknown until observed | inherit-parent unless a supported explicit effort is verified | Record rejected selection, replacement request, observed result and limits. |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| comment-sicko | inherit-parent | unknown until observed | inherit-parent unless a supported explicit effort is verified | Record rejected selection, replacement request, observed result and limits. |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nFor each assignment, record requested model and effort, observed model and effort, supported effort values and their source, worker identity, and fallback disclosure. Use `unknown` for information the operation does not expose. `inherit-parent` records a selection policy, not an observed model name. Do not silently convert a requested model into an actual model.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nSelect an explicit model only from current exposed tool documentation or observed enumeration results. If enumeration is missing, disclose that limit and retain `inherit-parent`. Documentation examples are not a list of available models. Use only an effort supported for the selected model by the current operation; do not carry an incompatible inherited effort into an explicit selection.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nIf a selected model or effort is rejected, retain the rejection. Use an authorized inherited or supported alternative only after disclosing the changed selection. If the task requires that exact selection, stop and record blocked. A fallback never proves the original selection succeeded.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nIdentical observed models mean no model diversity. Different roles can supply distinct instructions on the same model. Unknown observed models leave diversity unverified, even when requested model names differ. Different observed models support a model-diversity statement only for those observed assignments. Worker independence still requires successful spawn and result inspection.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nWhen workers are unavailable, apply an authorized role sequentially and label reduced capability. Preserve scope and permission boundaries. Do not claim independent review, background execution, or full worker support from sequential role switching.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nThe cached documentation from https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/agent-configuration/subagents describes inheritance and optional local agent configuration. It is context only. This package writes no local custom-agent configuration and claims no current host model or worker support from that documentation.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n# Workflow ownership\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nAn explicit pstack invocation gives pstack ownership of that workflow. An explicit Superpowers invocation gives Superpowers ownership. Do not run both controllers over the same assignment or let one silently replace the other'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s plan, review, or integration decisions.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nFor an unqualified request, D1 retains the already active workflow. With no active workflow, use host defaults and do not activate either orchestration mode automatically. If both pstack and Superpowers are explicitly requested, clarify ownership before launching workers or changing artifacts. Preserve the active controller across follow-ups unless the user changes ownership. A helper skill can advise the owner without becoming a second controller.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nThe owner assigns one coordinator. Workers produce isolated artifacts and evidence; the coordinator reviews and integrates them. Shared inventory and registry writes belong to the coordinator. Worker cancellation, stale results or unavailable tools do not transfer that ownership automatically.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nHost permissions and user authorization constrain every owner. Local structural tests, documented APIs and declared cases provide no host pass. Explicit reduced or blocked results remain visible beside the full expectation.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nname: principle-prove-it-works\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\ndescription: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Apply after completing a task, before declaring done. Verify against the real artifact (run the feature, read the actual value, inspect the diff), not a proxy, self-report, or '\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''it compiles.'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n# Prove It Works\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nVerify every task output by checking the real thing directly. Do not infer from proxies, self-reports, or \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"it compiles.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n**Why:** Unverified work has unknown correctness. Indirect verification (file mtimes, output freshness, agent self-reports, cached screenshots) feels cheaper than direct observation. Acting on a wrong inference costs far more than checking the source.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nCheck the real thing, not a proxy:\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Check process liveness directly, not indirectly through derived state\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Read the actual value, not a cached or derived representation\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- When verification fails, suspect the observation method before suspecting the system\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Script the check when you can\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nThe strongest proof is a deterministic script that re-runs the same comparison, not a one-time eyeball. Write the script, run it, and keep its output as an artifact a reviewer can re-run instead of trusting your word.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nKeep the artifact visible for the human. Commit it only for large or complex work where the trail has to be auditable later, like a big port or migration (the **show-me-your-work** skill).\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Session capabilities and permissions\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nUse the exposed tools and observed operations described in [host capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md). Run checks and retain artifacts only within the user'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s authorized scope and host permissions. If direct inspection or execution is unavailable or denied, state which outputs remain unverified and withhold completion for those outputs. A proposed script is not evidence that it ran.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nname: principle-separate-before-serializing-shared-state\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\ndescription: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Apply when concurrent actors might write to the same file, branch, key, or state object. Eliminate the sharing first; serialize structurally only when one shared writer is a real invariant.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n# Separate Before Serializing Shared State\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nWhen concurrent actors might share mutable state, first ask whether they need the same mutable object. If not, eliminate the sharing. When sharing is real, enforce serialization structurally: lockfiles, sequential phases, exclusive ownership. Instructions and conventions are not concurrency control.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n**Why:** Concurrent writes to shared state create race conditions that are intermittent, hard to reproduce, and expensive to debug.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n**Pattern:**\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n1. **Identify shared mutable state** (files both read and write, branches both push to, APIs both define and consume).\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n2. **Default: eliminate the shared write target.** Ask: do these actors need one canonical object, or are they publishing independent facts? Give each actor its own owned file, key, branch, or state directory, and merge only at the read/reporting boundary. Two workers writing their own `lastX` field into one `state.json` is still shared mutation. `indexer-state.json` + `metrics-state.json` is not.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n3. **Only when one shared write target is a real invariant, serialize access structurally** (lockfiles, sequential phases, single-writer actor, or atomic compare-and-swap). Treat \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"we need a lock\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" as a design smell to check, not as the default answer.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Session capabilities and permissions\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nBefore dispatching writers or changing shared state, check [session capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md) and follow the [assignment and integration contract](../../references/orchestration-contract.md). Assign distinct owned paths before writes start. Isolation does not grant permission to spawn workers, write files or perform external actions.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nIf execution or writes are unavailable or denied, state the missing requirement and provide the isolation design without claiming implementation. Authorized sequential work is reduced capability. A design or sequential run does not prove independent workers or prevention of concurrent races.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n    }\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  },\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"name\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"workflowRead\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"args\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"cmd\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"cat skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/investigation.md; sed -n '\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''200,310p'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\'' skills/poteto-mode/SKILL.md\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"workdir\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"max_output_tokens\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": 9000\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n    },\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n    \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"chunk_id\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"42d119\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"wall_time_seconds\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": 0.000010916,\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"exit_code\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": 0,\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"original_token_count\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": 3609,\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"# Investigation\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nYou own the answer. Plan, route, write.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nInvestigation requests are read-only. They produce a cited explanation or a recommendation, not a code change. Read the complete [poteto mode](../SKILL.md) and this playbook before applying it. Preserve its Investigation routing for how, why, are-we-sure and choice-between-alternatives questions.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nBefore source reads or delegation, discover [session capabilities](../../../references/host-capabilities.md) and follow [model roles](../../../references/model-roles.md) and the [assignment contract](../../../references/orchestration-contract.md). Use only exposed, authorized read operations. Inherit parent model and effort unless a supported explicit selection is observed. Requested models are not observed models. The routed skills own their prompt and source contracts; this playbook directly invokes no script helper.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n1. Route through the [how skill](../../how/SKILL.md). For motivation questions, also route through the [why skill](../../why/SKILL.md). Read their complete instructions and the prompts and source playbooks required by the chosen route. Keep every investigator and explainer read-only, including connector-backed reads. Inspect delegated findings before relying on them. If independent workers are unavailable or unauthorized, disclose reduced capability and apply the same roles sequentially. Name inaccessible, denied, failed and searched-empty sources separately. Limit the answer to inspected evidence. Missing required companions or no authorized relevant source reads blocks the dependent answer.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n2. Throughput checkpoint stays one line: `throughput checkpoint: n/a, read-only investigation`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n3. Produce the how-shaped output, Overview / Key Concepts / How It Works / Where Things Live / Gotchas, omitting sections only as how permits. For a decision between alternatives, produce a recommendation with a tradeoffs table. For motivation, preserve why confidence tiers, source coverage, contradictions and unknowns within the investigation output. Cite actual inspected sources. Accessible sources that support the answer are enough to deliver it without starting a product build or prototype.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n4. Apply the [unslop skill](../../unslop/SKILL.md) to the reply.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nNo PR, no babysit, no [architect](../../architect/SKILL.md) unless the investigation precedes a code change. If it does, hand back to the user and re-route to [Bug fix](bug-fix.md) or [Feature](feature.md). That handoff does not authorize implementation, commits, PR creation or other writes. The Investigation exception takes precedence over the mode index generic Opening a PR rule. Do not install tools, change credentials or modify source state to obtain evidence.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nReply with the investigation output. For are-we-sure answers, include your real judgment with reasons. Push back if the premise is wrong under the mode Autonomy rules. Broad autonomy never overrides user authorization or host permissions. Disclose unperformed reads, uncertain conclusions and reduced roles; structural checks establish no runtime behavior or independent-worker success.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Usage or runtime errors exit 1.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Ledger rows replace the same key without enforcing verifier precedence.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  The caller owns precedence, append-only attempt history, exact SHA syntax, artifact inspection, terminal states, permissions and current-head filtering.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  The helper never spawns, waits or wakes workers.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Stale locks require ownership review, and `--force` requires explicit takeover permission even for a dead holder.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Frontier discovery uses local git and gt in the stack owner'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s clone and fails on unavailable metadata or pin mismatch.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Do not substitute this binding for the gt-free Autopilot stack workflow.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Atomic rename is not a crash-durability or adversarial-concurrency guarantee.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Babysit and Shipping use the GitHub-specific read-only [watcher](scripts/watch-pr/watch-pr) through their complete playbooks.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  From the package root bind `bun --no-install skills/poteto-mode/scripts/watch-pr/watch-pr` with explicit `--owner`, `--repo`, PR selection, `--head <number=full-40-character-head>` for every watched member, `--max-polls <remaining-count>` and `--timeout <remaining-seconds>`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Freeze queued order with `--queued-stack --stack-prs <bottom-to-top-list>`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Connected mode uses `--stack`, mutually exclusive with queued mode. `check` adds `--status-only`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Inspect NDJSON schemaVersion 1 kind, terminal and reason.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Exit 0 includes status-only and proves no readiness by itself.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Codes 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7 and 64 identify conflicts, threads, checks, timeout, merge gates, query failures and usage.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  A changed head, missing authentication or transport, or exhausted poll-budget blocks the result.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Queued WAITING with reason kind merge-queue is non-terminal.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Babysit reports the frontier merge-ready and stops that watcher.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Shipping waits for observed merge under its distinct rule.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Carry budgets across re-arms and retain an observed stop or deadline binding for a hard wall-clock limit.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  The helper deadline is checked between polling work.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  Do not add a second sleep loop or treat GitHub output as Origin evidence.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Cleanup uses the [audit wrapper](scripts/worktree-audit.sh) and [implementation](scripts/worktree-audit.py) through its playbook with an explicit local `--repo` and verified `--base`, plus authorized `--history-map` and `--pr-state` exports when available. The helper returns logical bytes and evidence buckets, performs no fetch or deletion, and leaves unknown facts as holds. A bucket never grants deletion permission. Recheck actual usage and ignored files before separately authorized removal.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nThe [bootstrap](scripts/bootstrap.ts), [manifest](scripts/package.json) and [lock](scripts/bun.lock) require exact commander 14.0.0, bun-types 1.3.14 and typescript 7.0.2 metadata before dependency-backed CLI execution. Bootstrap fails setup-required and never installs. Run helpers with Bun runtime auto-install disabled. Missing dependencies remain blocked. The isolated tested snapshot only supports that snapshot, not this caller, the current checkout or either host. [Helper setup](../../docs/testing/helper-setup.md) states the exact-copy validation boundary and retains historical setup evidence. It does not authorize installation or transfer a historical pass to this caller. Any future setup needs fresh authorization for an exact writable copy, registry, disabled lifecycle scripts and task-owned cache. Never install in an operative invocation or bypass bootstrap.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nDelivery reads [Opening a PR](playbooks/opening-a-pr.md) in full and retains its review, companion, commit, push, draft or ready authorization and actual-result gates. A local draft is not a created PR. An exposed built-in PR tool owns operations it covers, and a failed tool does not authorize a CLI bypass. Opening alone never starts Babysit. A read-only question stays in Investigation, with no PR; its specific exception takes precedence over the generic delivery route. Prototype and planning handoffs grant no implementation or external permission. Missing create-skill, deslop, matching controls or independent review remains an explicit blocked gate even when a reduced local artifact is useful.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Writing the reply\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nWrite the reply clean as you draft it. A cleanup pass after drafting does not remove these patterns.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Short declarative sentences.** One thought per sentence, ended with a period.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **No long-dash character anywhere.** Write a file-list bullet as a sentence (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"`main.js` owns persistence and the IPC handlers\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\") and a bold section header as its own sentence (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"**Verification.** End to end via CDP\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\").\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **A colon as a mid-sentence connector is also out** (unslop rule 14). A colon before a list is fine.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Terse is not an excuse to drop content.** Short sentences, but every section the playbook'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s reply names stays: details, tradeoffs, choices, open decisions.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Frame impact for the consumer and the maintainer.** Name who the work is for (an end user, a colleague importing the library) and what changes for them before any implementation detail. Then what the next engineer who owns this code inherits. If you can'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''t say what either would notice, the work or the explanation is off.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Never fabricate a link, citation, or transcript reference.** Link only artifacts you produced or read this session.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Every claim carries its evidence or its label in the same sentence.** Measured, inferred, or guess. A prediction or an unseen cause is a guess. Never hand the human a check you could run.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nEvery playbook ends with a reply written this way. Link to the actual PR you read or created when authorized. Never claim a draft is a created PR. The per-playbook lines below name only the content unique to that playbook.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Comments\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nComments follow the same rule as the reply. Write them clean as you go. Keep a comment only for a non-obvious *why* the code can'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''t show. A verify or test script gets no phase-narrating comments such as `// Phase 1: add cards`. The assertion or log string documents the step, as in `assert(ok, '\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''persisted across restart'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\'')`. This applies to every file you produce, including the delegate'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s diff.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Playbooks\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nOpen an available todo list, or an authorized local Markdown checklist if no todo operation exists. Record that reduced tool binding. Its first items are the matched playbook'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s steps, copied in verbatim, before any task-specific todos. A step you choose not to do stays in the list with a one-line `skip: <reason>`. Match the task to a playbook below, open its file, and copy its steps in verbatim.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nA large or cross-cutting effort (a migration across many call sites, an ambitious multi-part change), or work the user steps away from to trust later, routes to the **figure-it-out** skill even when a narrower playbook like Feature fits. Use **figure-it-out** whenever no bundled playbook fits. It designs a bespoke, rigorous playbook for the task. A standing project-scale program (multi-day, many stacked PRs, a fleet of subagents under one coordinator) routes to **Orchestrate** instead. figure-it-out designs one bespoke run, orchestrate runs the program.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Investigation.** Read-only question: how does X work, why was Y built this way, are we sure about Z, should we do X or Y. `playbooks/investigation.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Bug fix.** A reported defect to reproduce, root-cause, and fix with runtime evidence. `playbooks/bug-fix.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Perf issue.** A measured slowness to trace and improve against a baseline. `playbooks/perf-issue.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Hillclimb.** Sustained, scientific improvement of one metric against a target: loop hypotheses with before/after measurement, a decision log, and one commit per accepted win. Distinct from Perf issue, which is a one-off fix. `playbooks/hillclimb.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Runtime forensics.** Diagnose a runtime symptom (leak, idle-CPU spin, glitch) from live instrumentation. The deliverable is a diagnosis, not a fix. `playbooks/runtime-forensics.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Trace forensics.** Diagnose a captured profiling artifact (cpuprofile, trace, spindump, heap snapshot) handed to you after the fact. The deliverable is a diagnosis, not a fix. `playbooks/trace-forensics.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Feature.** New or changed behavior, built from a named data shape. `playbooks/feature.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Refactoring.** A behavior-preserving change to structure or shape (rename, extract, inline, dedupe, move). `playbooks/refactoring.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Prototype.** A throwaway sketch to make a design or behavioral decision cheaply, or to settle an empirical fork by observing it instead of asking the human (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"prototype\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"mock it up\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"try this layout\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"sketch it to decide\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"). `playbooks/prototype.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Visual parity.** Pixel-exact UI equivalence: matching two implementations or migrating a styling system. `playbooks/visual-parity.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Authoring or modifying a skill.** Writing or editing a SKILL.md. `playbooks/authoring-a-skill.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Eval.** Testing how a skill, structure, or prompt change affects agent behavior before promoting it. `playbooks/eval.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Babysit.** Driving a PR or a stack to merge-ready: conflicts, review threads, CI. `playbooks/babysit.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Shipping.** The half after Babysit. Independently verifying a green stack, then landing the contiguous verified run bottom-up through `gh` by default or Origin when its CLI is available. `playbooks/shipping.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Autonomous run.** A long task to drive to completion without stopping (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"run until done\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"/loop until X\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"). `playbooks/autonomous-run.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Orchestrate.** A standing project handed to one coordinator chat: multi-day, many stacked PRs, dozens to hundreds of subagents, minimal human turns (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"run this whole project\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"own this migration until it lands\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"). Distinct from Autonomous run, which drives one task to a predicate. Work one agent could finish inside the session'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s budget routes there, not here, however program-shaped the phrasing sounds. `playbooks/orchestrate.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Autopilot-full.** A queue of independent PRs run to merged with full autonomy. One owner per PR carries build through merge, and the root swarm-verifies each PR before its owner merges (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"autopilot this queue\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"full autopilot\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", one-owner-per-PR programs). `playbooks/autopilot-full.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Autopilot-stack.** A queue of changes built and verified with full autonomy, delivered as one linear reviewed base-branch stack the operator lands (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"autopilot-stack\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"stack them, don'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''t ship\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"build the stack, I'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''ll land it\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"). `playbooks/autopilot-stack.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Session pickup.** Resuming or taking over a prior agent'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s in-flight work from a transcript, cloud-agent URL, or pushed branch. `playbooks/session-pickup.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Pause safely.** Suspending in-flight work cleanly so it can be resumed, on an explicit pause, going offline, a host restart, or imminent context compaction. The complement to Session pickup. Full steps: `playbooks/pause-safely.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Multi-phase or multi-PR plan.** Work that spans phases or stacked PRs. `playbooks/multi-phase-plan.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Worktree and simulator cleanup.** Reclaiming local disk by pruning merged or abandoned git worktrees and stale iOS simulators (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"what'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s using my disk\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"clean up worktrees\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"prune safe-to-prune worktrees\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"free up space\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"delete old simulators\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"). `playbooks/worktree-cleanup.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Opening a PR.** Invoked at the end of every other playbook. `playbooks/opening-a-pr.md`.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n    }\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  },\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n  {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n skills/how/SKILL.md skills/principle-model-the-domain/SKILL.md\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"workdir\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\":      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"max_output_tokens\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": 7000\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n    },\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"62e865\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"wall_time_seconds\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": 0.000005792,\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"exit_code\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": 0,\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"original_token_count\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": 1689,\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n      \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"---\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nname: how\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\ndescription: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Use for \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"how does X work\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", code walkthroughs before changing something, and placement / ownership / layering questions (\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"where should this live\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"which package owns this\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"is this the right layer\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"). Explains subsystem architecture, runtime flow, onboarding mental models. Use why for motivation.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n# How\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nExplore the codebase to answer \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"how does X work?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" questions. Produce architectural explanations at the level of a senior engineer onboarding onto a subsystem, enough to build a working mental model, not so much that it reads like annotated source code.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nBefore dispatch, read [session capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md), [model roles](../../references/model-roles.md), and the [assignment contract](../../references/orchestration-contract.md). Bind worker operations to tools actually exposed and authorized in this session. Request the inherited parent model and effort unless a supported explicit selection is observed. Retain rejections and disclose replacements; an unavailable required selection is blocked. Record requested and observed selections separately, using unknown for unobserved values.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nKeep explorers and explainers read-only. Worker assignments record their role, distinct angle, exact input revision, identity and inspected results under the assignment contract. Reading a prompt does not register a worker. If independent workers are unavailable or unauthorized, disclose reduced capability and perform the same exploration and explanation roles sequentially. Never claim parallel or independent exploration from sequential work. If source access is unavailable or denied, report the gap and limit the explanation to inspected evidence.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Step 1. Assess Complexity\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nIf the scope is ambiguous, state your interpretation and explore. The user can redirect.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Simple** (a single module, a small utility, a narrow question such as \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"how does function X work\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"): no explorers. One explainer explores and explains in a single pass. Go to Step 2b.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- **Complex** (a subsystem spanning multiple files or services, a cross-cutting feature, a full architectural overview): use parallel explorers when available, then hand off to the explainer. Go to Step 2a.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nWhen in doubt, take the simple path.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Step 2a. Explore (complex questions only)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nDecompose the question into 2 to 4 exploration angles, each a distinct slice of the subsystem. Dispatch all explorers together through an observed worker binding, with distinct assignments:\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Role: how explorer\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Model and effort: follow the model-role contract\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Access: read-only\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nEach explorer gets the prompt in `references/explorer-prompt.md` with its angle filled in. If workers are unavailable, explore those distinct angles sequentially and retain separate findings. Then go to Step 3.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Step 2b. Direct Explain (simple questions)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nAssign one explainer worker that explores and explains in one pass:\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Role: how explainer\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Model and effort: follow the model-role contract\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Access: read-only\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nBuild its prompt from `references/explainer-prompt.md` without the explorer-findings section. The explainer reads and traces the source itself. If workers are unavailable, apply this role sequentially. Go to Step 4.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Step 3. Synthesize (complex questions only)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nOnce all explorer results have been inspected, assign one explainer worker to synthesize their findings into one explanation:\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Role: how explainer\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Model and effort: follow the model-role contract\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Access: read-only\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nBuild its prompt from `references/explainer-prompt.md` with every explorer'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s findings filled in. In reduced capability, synthesize the sequential findings under the same read-only role.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Step 4. Present\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nPresent the explainer'\\\\''\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\'''\\\\''s output to the user. Light edits for clarity or context from the conversation are fine. Do not substantially rewrite it.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Output Format\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nThe explanation uses the sections defined in `references/explainer-prompt.md`, dropping any that do not apply: Overview, Key Concepts, How It Works, Where Things Live, Gotchas.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nname: principle-model-the-domain\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\ndescription: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Apply when writing stateful logic, or when code branches a lot or repeats a shape assumption across files. Encode the domain in a structure instead of scattered conditionals.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n---\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n# Model the Domain\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nEncode the real domain in a data structure instead of scattering it across conditionals.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n**Why:** Scattered booleans, repeated shape assumptions, and branching spread across files are accidental complexity. A structure that matches the domain makes invalid states unrepresentable and deletes branches. Choosing it at write time is cheap. Recovering it later reads as a refactor and gets deferred.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n**Reach for structures like these:**\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- A state machine instead of scattered booleans, phases, or lifecycle checks.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- A typed object/model instead of loose parameters or repeated shape assumptions.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- A map, registry, lookup table, or discriminated union instead of branching spread across files.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- A reducer or command/event model instead of ad hoc state mutations.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- A module organized around one body of domain knowledge instead of a sequence such as load, validate, transform, and save. Execution order is not ownership.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- A small module boundary that gathers repeated behavior, ownership, or invariants.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- A queue, cache, index, graph/tree, or normalized collection where the data access pattern calls for it.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Any other structure that fits. When none fits, work out what the code must never allow and how the data gets read, then find the structure that encodes exactly that.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nDo not force an abstraction. Prefer boring code if the current shape is already clear, local, and unlikely to grow. Be skeptical of an abstraction that adds indirection without removing branches, duplicated rules, invalid states, or lifecycle risk.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nThe sign that you skipped this is a new feature that grows an existing if/else chain by one more branch, or a second boolean that must stay in sync with the first. Temporal decomposition is another sign. Phase-named modules repeat the same domain rules across steps.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Session capabilities and authorization\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nFollow [session capability discovery](../../references/host-capabilities.md) for source reads, writes and execution. Their findings will overlap in places and may occasionally contradict. Reconcile them. Merge overlapping descriptions, resolve contradictions by checking the code yourself, and combine the separate slices into a unified picture.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nWrite an explanation a senior engineer unfamiliar with this area could read and walk away with a solid mental model, understanding the architecture well enough to start working in it confidently.\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nYou have read-only access to the codebase to check anything, clarify a detail, or fill a gap.       "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Investigation\n\nYou own the answer. Plan, route, write.\n\nInvestigation requests are read-only. They produce a cited explanation or a recommendation, not a code change. Read the complete [poteto mode](../SKILL.md) and this playbook before applying it. Preserve its Investigation routing for how, why, are-we-sure and choice-between-alternatives questions.\n\nBefore source reads or delegation, discover [session capabilities](../../../references/host-capabilities.md) and follow [model roles](../../../references/model-roles.md) and the [assignment contract](../../../references/orchestration-contract.md). Use only exposed, authorized read operations. Inherit parent model and effort unless a supported explicit selection is observed. Requested models are not observed models. The routed skills own their prompt and source contracts; this playbook directly invokes no script helper.\n\n1. Route through the [how skill](../../how/SKILL.md). For motivation questions, also route through the [why skill](../../why/SKILL.md). Read their complete instructions and the prompts and source playbooks required by the chosen route. Keep every investigator and explainer read-only, including connector-backed reads. Inspect delegated findings before relying on them. If independent workers are unavailable or unauthorized, disclose reduced capability and apply the same roles sequentially. Name inaccessible, denied, failed and searched-empty sources separately. Limit the answer to inspected evidence. Missing required companions or no authorized relevant source reads blocks the dependent answer.\n2. Throughput checkpoint stays one line: `throughput checkpoint: n/a, read-only investigation`.\n3. Produce the how-shaped output, Overview / Key Concepts / How It Works / Where Things Live / Gotchas, omitting sections only as how permits. For a decision between alternatives, produce a recommendation with a tradeoffs table. For motivation, preserve why confidence tiers, source coverage, contradictions and unknowns within the investigation output. Cite actual inspected sources. Accessible sources that support the answer are enough to deliver it without starting a product build or prototype.\n4. Apply the [unslop skill](../../unslop/SKILL.md) to the reply.\n\nNo PR, no babysit, no [architect](../../architect/SKILL.md) unless the investigation precedes a code change. If it does, hand back to the user and re-route to [Bug fix](bug-fix.md) or [Feature](feature.md). That handoff does not authorize implementation, commits, PR creation or other writes. The Investigation exception takes precedence over the mode index generic Opening a PR rule. Do not install tools, change credentials or modify source state to obtain evidence.\n\nReply with the investigation output. For are-we-sure answers, include your real judgment with reasons. Push back if the premise is wrong under the mode Autonomy rules. Broad autonomy never overrides user authorization or host permissions. Disclose unperformed reads, uncertain conclusions and reduced roles; structural checks establish no runtime behavior or independent-worker success.\n\nthroughput checkpoint: n/a, read-only investigation\nDirect assigned explainer; nested delegation forbidden. No todo operation exposed, local checklist used.\n"
